# Skills · T · part 25

920 entries, tdd-agent to tdd-refactor. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **tdd-agent**: Implements issues test-first: writes failing tests, commits them, then implements the minimum code to pass, then refactors. The failing-test commit is mandatory and never skipped. Trigger on: any request to implement an issue, write code for a feature, or fix a bug. Requires acceptance criteria — asks for them if absent. ([one-aalam/agentic-dev-skills](https://github.com/one-aalam/agentic-dev-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/tdd-agent))
- **tdd-agent**: Test-driven development as distinct agentic phases: Red (write failing tests), Green (implement until tests pass), Refactor (improve without breaking tests). Use when building features or fixing bugs where correctness is verifiable via tests. Covers phase contracts, test-first design, coverage gates, and harness integration. Complements validation (which covers general test design). ([thistleknot/skills](https://github.com/thistleknot/skills/tree/HEAD/tdd-agent))

- **tdd-code-skeleton**: TDD前期根据需求/设计文档生成代码骨架，含详细类/方法注释与TODO，为编写单元测试做准备。触发关键词：「生成代码框架」「代码骨架」「code skeleton」「TDD框架生成」「类框架」「方法存根」「stub生成」「按设计文档生成代码」「骨架代码」，或TDD中需要先生成实现框架再写测试。支持 Java、C++、Scala、Python、Go、Rust、JavaScript、TypeScript 八种语言，按检测结果只加载对应 references。**支持断点续作**：启动时先在目标目录查找 `*-tdd-skeleton-tasks.md` 规划文件；若存在且结构完整则按进度续作（仅对 pending 任务重新派发），不重新走设计阶段。**主流程**：设计阶段由独立 subagent 完成（解析文档、识别类、规划 batch、落盘完整任务清单到原始设计文档同级目录），主上下文只收批次列表以保留窗口；每个生成 subagent 承担 5~10 个类（按规模缩放，方法多偏 5、方法少偏 10），每 wave 5~8 个 batch 并行；每 wave 回写状态后做完整性反思，对 pending 循环补发直到收敛。**全流程自动化**：设计落盘后自动推进到语言检测、批次派发、反思收敛、交付，仅在硬冲突（文档矛盾、推断项高风险、连续补发失败）时暂停。 ([wangbinquan/skills](https://github.com/wangbinquan/skills/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/tdd-code-skeleton) · ★2)

- **tdd-green**: TDD의 Green 단계를 수행한다. `tdd-red` 가 만든 실패(Red) 테스트를, **하나씩 통과시키는 최소한의 구현 코드**만 작성하여 초록(Green)으로 바꾼다. 입력은 GitHub 이슈 번호이며 `docs/features/{feature}/issue-{N}.md` 의 시그니처·시나리오·AC를 기준으로 삼는다. 실행 순서는 (1) `pnpm test` 로 실패 목록 확인 → (2) collect 실패면 시그니처만 있는 stub 생성 → (3) `docs/design-system/` 디자인 컨텍스트 로드 → (4) 첫 실패 테스트를 통과시키는 최소 코드 작성 → (5) 재실행·회귀 확인(최대 5회 피드백 루프) → (6) issue 체크박스 `[x]` → (7) 다음 실패로 반복 → (8) 전체 통과 후 커버리지 측정(참고용) → (9) 요약. **테스트 파일은 절대 수정하지 않고, 테스트에 없는 기능·불필요한 추상화·디자인 문서에 없는 시각 요소는 만들지 않는다.** Use when (1) 사용자가 `/tdd-green &lt;이슈번호&gt;` 명령을 쓸 때, (2) "Green 단계", "테스트 통과시켜줘", "최소 구현", "Red 끝났으니 구현해줘"… ([Central-MakeUs/moyeo-client](https://github.com/Central-MakeUs/moyeo-client/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/tdd-green) · ★1)

Not for planning a feature (use tdd-guardian-planner), and not for implementing many work items in one batch. - **tdd-guardian-init**: Detect every test lane in this repository — unit, integration, e2e, contract — verify each by dry-run probe, and write `.claude/tdd-guardian/config.json`. Reads CI config first, since that is what maintainers actually run. ([xiaolai/tdd-guardian-for-claude](https://github.com/xiaolai/tdd-guardian-for-claude/tree/HEAD/codex/skills/tdd-guardian-init) · ★14)

- **tdd-guardian-mutation-auditor**: Validate test robustness using mutation testing and report every surviving mutant with the test that would kill it, including whether the mutation score meets the kill-rate threshold and whether the mutation tool is installed. Report-only — the implementer writes the tests. Not for checking coverage thresholds (use tdd-guardian-coverage-auditor) or for writing the tests that kill survivors. ([xiaolai/tdd-guardian-for-claude](https://github.com/xiaolai/tdd-guardian-for-claude/tree/HEAD/codex/skills/tdd-guardian-mutation-auditor) · ★14)
- **tdd-guardian-mutation-gate**: Mutation testing gate: per-language tools (Stryker, PIT, mutmut), weak assertions. ([xiaolai/tdd-guardian-for-claude](https://github.com/xiaolai/tdd-guardian-for-claude/tree/HEAD/codex/skills/tdd-guardian-mutation-gate) · ★14)
- **tdd-guardian-plan**: Break a TDD task into work items, acceptance criteria, and required test targets, using the tdd-guardian planner, and save the plan under .claude/tdd-guardian/. Does NOT write code or tests. ([xiaolai/tdd-guardian-for-claude](https://github.com/xiaolai/tdd-guardian-for-claude/tree/HEAD/codex/skills/tdd-guardian-plan) · ★14)
- **tdd-guardian-planner**: Break a request — a new feature or a refactor — into implementation work items with explicit acceptance criteria, risks, and test targets, before any code is written. Not for implementing code (use tdd-guardian-implementer) or for coverage questions (use tdd-guardian-coverage-auditor). ([xiaolai/tdd-guardian-for-claude](https://github.com/xiaolai/tdd-guardian-for-claude/tree/HEAD/codex/skills/tdd-guardian-planner) · ★14)
- **tdd-guardian-policy-core**: Core TDD policy: plan-first development, assertion and spec levels, completion gates. ([xiaolai/tdd-guardian-for-claude](https://github.com/xiaolai/tdd-guardian-for-claude/tree/HEAD/codex/skills/tdd-guardian-policy-core) · ★14)
- **tdd-guardian-probe**: Dry-run every configured lane to verify its command resolves and discovers tests, without executing the suites. Read-only; runs no tests and writes no files. ([xiaolai/tdd-guardian-for-claude](https://github.com/xiaolai/tdd-guardian-for-claude/tree/HEAD/codex/skills/tdd-guardian-probe) · ★14)
- **tdd-guardian-review**: Run the tdd-guardian-reviewer skill for a final code + test-quality review. ([xiaolai/tdd-guardian-for-claude](https://github.com/xiaolai/tdd-guardian-for-claude/tree/HEAD/codex/skills/tdd-guardian-review) · ★14)

- **tdd-guardian-reviewer**: Final reviewer that audits code quality, test quality (wiring vs behavior), and coverage gaps; also use it to flag the wiring-only tests in a specific test file. - **tdd-guardian-spec-adversary**: Attack a test matrix before any implementation exists — find the wrong implementation that would pass every case, such as one that skips a state transition or breaks an invariant. A compile, type, import, syntax or setup error, a missing file or a runner finding no tests is NOT RED; a minimal stub makes the test compile first.
